# Tenable Security Inspector

Portable contract for Tenable scan, sensor, access-control, and vulnerability-management assessments.

## Purpose

Assess Tenable scan-program, sensor, access-control, and vulnerability-management posture across Vulnerability Management and optional Security Center tenants.

## Design guidance

Keep the two Tenable products as distinct evidence sources. Export completion requires every reported chunk, and a failed poll, missing chunk, unevaluable record, repeated page, or configured cap must remain incomplete rather than becoming an empty result.

## Shared integration contract

This specification requires [shared integration contract version 1.1](./integration-contract.md). The raw contract is available at https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ethanolivertroy/grclanker/main/specs/integration-contract.md.

## Known runtime gaps

- Vulnerability Management and Security Center inventories remain separate sources; a Security Center equivalent adds a suffixed finding and never replaces the cloud control.
- Export status, every available chunk, failed chunks, unevaluable records, configured chunk caps, and poll failures contribute to completeness before any finding count is evaluated.
- A 2xx document missing the endpoint's documented collection member is unreadable rather than an empty inventory.
- Tenable Security Center contributes schedule, scanner, feed, and user equivalents only; cloud-only exports and access-group controls remain manual for Security Center-only tenants.
- TENABLE-07-SC can pass from healthy Security Center scanner rows even when the scanner inventory is truncated, so unread scanner rows are not reflected in that result.
- TENABLE-11 can pass from clean delivered permission rows when permissions or user-group inventory is truncated; a broad permission in delivered rows still fails, but unread rows are not reflected.
- TENABLE-19 can pass when external export jobs observed on at least two days come from a truncated export-job listing, so unread jobs are not reflected.

## Tools

| Tool | Purpose | Finding IDs | Result shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tenable_check_access` | Validate read-only Tenable Vulnerability Management (cloud or FedRAMP) and optional Tenable Security Center access across scans, templates, exports, sensors, networks, exclusions, credentials, users, permissions, audit log, and tags, reporting the role each refused surface needs. | None | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `tenable_assess_scan_program` | Assess Tenable scan policy configuration, scan schedule discipline, credentialed scan ratio, scan exclusions, compliance audit templates, and deprecated target groups (spec controls 1, 2, 4, 13, 17, 20) plus the Security Center schedule equivalent. | `TENABLE-01`, `TENABLE-02`, `TENABLE-04`, `TENABLE-13`, `TENABLE-17`, `TENABLE-20` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `tenable_assess_sensor_coverage` | Assess Tenable asset discovery coverage, agent deployment and grouping, linked scanner health, plugin currency, network zones, and asset tagging (spec controls 3, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 16) plus the Security Center scanner equivalent. | `TENABLE-03`, `TENABLE-05`, `TENABLE-06`, `TENABLE-07`, `TENABLE-08`, `TENABLE-09`, `TENABLE-16` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `tenable_assess_access_control` | Assess Tenable user roles, MFA and SAML enforcement, API key usage, access control permissions and deprecated access groups, managed credential hygiene, and activity log review (spec controls 10, 11, 12, 18) plus the Security Center user equivalent. | `TENABLE-10`, `TENABLE-11`, `TENABLE-12`, `TENABLE-18` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `tenable_assess_vulnerability_management` | Run the documented vulnerability and asset exports to assess VPR prioritization coverage, severity SLA backlog and remediation times, and export automation evidence (spec controls 14, 15, 19). | `TENABLE-14`, `TENABLE-15`, `TENABLE-19` | A text result whose structured details preserve the runtime assessment or access-check object byte-for-byte. |
| `tenable_export_audit_bundle` | Export a Tenable audit package with raw API snapshots (core_data), normalized findings (analysis), executive summary, unified compliance matrix, per-framework reports (compliance), QUICK_REFERENCE.md, _errors.log on partial collection, and a zip archive paired with the output directory. | `TENABLE-01`, `TENABLE-02`, `TENABLE-03`, `TENABLE-04`, `TENABLE-05`, `TENABLE-06`, `TENABLE-07`, `TENABLE-08`, `TENABLE-09`, `TENABLE-10`, `TENABLE-11`, `TENABLE-12`, `TENABLE-13`, `TENABLE-14`, `TENABLE-15`, `TENABLE-16`, `TENABLE-17`, `TENABLE-18`, `TENABLE-19`, `TENABLE-20` | A text result plus output directory, paired archive path, file count, finding count, and collection-error count. |

### Parameters

#### `tenable_check_access`

| Parameter | Kind | Required | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `access_key` | string | no | Tenable Vulnerability Management API access key. Defaults to TENABLE_ACCESS_KEY or the config file. |
| `secret_key` | string | no | Tenable Vulnerability Management API secret key. Defaults to TENABLE_SECRET_KEY or the config file. |
| `url` | string | no | Platform URL: https://cloud.tenable.com (default), https://fedcloud.tenable.com for FedRAMP, or a Tenable Security Center host. Defaults to TENABLE_URL. |
| `sc_url` | string | no | Optional Tenable Security Center URL assessed alongside the cloud tenant. Defaults to TENABLE_SC_URL. |
| `sc_access_key` | string | no | Tenable Security Center API access key (x-apikey). Defaults to TENABLE_SC_ACCESS_KEY. |
| `sc_secret_key` | string | no | Tenable Security Center API secret key (x-apikey). Defaults to TENABLE_SC_SECRET_KEY. |
| `config_file` | string | no | Optional YAML or JSON config file with access_key, secret_key, url, sc_url, sc_access_key, and sc_secret_key. Defaults to TENABLE_CONFIG_FILE or ~/.tenable/config.yaml. |
| `timeout_seconds` | number | no | HTTP timeout in seconds. Defaults to 30. |

## Authentication

Supported modes:

- Tenable Vulnerability Management X-ApiKeys access/secret pair
- Tenable Security Center x-apikey access/secret pair

Credential precedence, highest first:

1. Explicit tool arguments
2. TENABLE_* environment variables
3. Explicit or default Tenable YAML/JSON config

Environment variables: `TENABLE_CONFIG_FILE`, `TENABLE_URL`, `TENABLE_BASE_URL`, `TENABLE_ACCESS_KEY`, `TENABLE_SECRET_KEY`, `TENABLE_SC_URL`, `TENABLE_SC_ACCESS_KEY`, `TENABLE_SC_SECRET_KEY`, `TENABLE_TIMEOUT`

Configuration locations: ~/.tenable/config.yaml, Explicit path from config_file or TENABLE_CONFIG_FILE

Credential and deployment variants: cloud.tenable.com, fedcloud.tenable.com, Tenable Security Center URL, Simultaneous VM and Security Center tenants

Configuration fields: `url`, `base_url`, `access_key`, `accessKey`, `secret_key`, `secretKey`, `sc_url`, `sc_access_key`, `sc_secret_key`, `timeout_seconds`

Malformed configuration: Reject malformed or ambiguous configuration before any request; never echo credential values.

## Permissions

| Kind | Permission, role, or plan | Unlocks | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| role | `Administrator` | `server-properties`, `scans`, `scan-details`, `policies`, `policy-details`, `scan-templates`, `scanners`, `agents`, `agent-groups`, `networks`, `exclusions`, `credentials`, `users`, `groups`, `roles`, `permissions`, `access-groups`, `audit-events`, `tag-categories`, `tag-values`, `target-groups`, `vuln-export-jobs`, `asset-export-jobs`, `asset-export`, `asset-export-status`, `asset-export-chunks`, `vuln-export`, `vuln-export-status`, `vuln-export-chunks`, `sc-current-user`, `sc-scans`, `sc-scan-results`, `sc-scanners`, `sc-users`, `sc-feed` | Full inventory and administrative metadata visibility. |
| role | `Scan Manager plus broad access-group membership` | `scans`, `scan-details`, `policies`, `policy-details`, `scan-templates`, `scanners`, `agents`, `agent-groups`, `networks`, `exclusions`, `credentials`, `target-groups` |  |

## Pagination

| Surfaces | Cursor or marker | Page size | Item cap | Page cap | Total semantics | Stop conditions |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `agents`, `networks`, `exclusions`, `credentials`, `access-groups`, `audit-events`, `tag-categories`, `tag-values` | `offset`, `pagination.total` | 1000 | caller limit | 200 | pagination.total is authoritative when present; without it a short page establishes exhaustion. | Empty or short page; Reported total reached; 200-page cap; Repeated first-record key |
| `asset-export-chunks`, `vuln-export-chunks` | `chunks_available`, `chunks_failed`, `total_chunks` | service default | 50 | none | Completion requires a FINISHED export, every available chunk fetched, no failed chunk, no unevaluable record, and total_chunks accounted for. | Every available chunk downloaded; Configured max_chunks reached; Chunk failure; Export ERROR or CANCELLED; Poll timeout |

## Rate limits

| Scope | Documented limit | Retry headers | Retryable statuses | Policy |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Tenable Security Inspector | Tenable Vulnerability Management documents API concurrency and rate limits per endpoint and account; Security Center limits are deployment-specific. | `Retry-After`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining`, `X-RateLimit-Limit` | 429, 500, 502, 503, 504 | Retry four times with Retry-After when present and bounded exponential backoff otherwise; export polling uses a separate five-minute deadline. |

### Primitive decision inputs

Every primitive is read from the named vendor surface or collector state before evidence lists are rendered or capped. Null and missing retain unavailable semantics; they are not empty inventories, false values, or zero counts.

| Finding | Input | Portable definition |
|---|---|---|
| `TENABLE-01` | `tenable_01_scan_policy_configuration_sources_readable` | Semantic owner: `TENABLE-01.tenable_01_scan_policy_configuration_sources_readable`. Type/domain: boolean. Compared literal domain: true. Source/owner: Tenable collector projection from `GET /policies` (policies), `GET /policies/{policy_id}` (policy-details). Completeness/sample semantics: The primitive is calculated from the uncapped collector state before any 25-item finding preview or export sample; list cardinalities therefore refer to every item the collector obtained. Null/missing meaning: the named source did not establish this primitive; null or absence cannot independently satisfy a passing rule. It feeds executable derived facts `tenable_01_branch_02_matches`, `tenable_01_branch_05_matches`. Portable meaning: Boolean set from the named source read results before any finding is created. True only when every response and required field used by TENABLE-01 is readable. |

Finding previews and exported samples never establish source cardinality. Exact source-state effects: `policies`: false on truncated, error, denied, not-collected, not-configured, missing-required-field; other failure modes do not change this fact; `policy-details`: false on truncated, error, denied, not-collected, not-configured, missing-required-field; other failure modes do not change this fact. Null/missing meaning: the named source did not establish this primitive; null or absence cannot independently satisfy a passing rule. It feeds executable derived facts `tenable_01_branch_04_matches`, `tenable_01_branch_05_matches`. Portable meaning: tenable_01_scan_policy_configuration_population_complete is true only after policies, policy-details each completed and returned every field required by this check. ### Ordered decision rules

Rules are evaluated from lowest order number to highest. The first matching condition determines the finding status; later rules are not evaluated.

A `matches` condition performs a regular-expression search; anchors are required for whole-value matching, and an `i` flag requests case-insensitive matching. A `ratio` condition divides the numerator by the denominator, applies the declared scale, and rounds to the declared decimal places by choosing the nearest value with exact half cases rounded toward positive infinity; a zero, null, or missing denominator does not match.

## Collection states

| State | Required rendering |
|---|---|
| complete | complete: proven API exhaustion or a successful single-object read. |
| truncated | truncated: preserve seen and total when available plus the exact stop reason. |
| unreadable | unreadable: render data and counts as null and retain a scrubbed error envelope. |
| denied | denied: render null evidence with the endpoint and HTTP status, never an empty inventory. |
| not requested | not_requested: identify the unreadable parent dependency and do not invent an HTTP status. |
| not configured | not_configured: identify the absent optional feature or credential without treating it as compliant. |

## Integration-specific scrubbing

Shared contract version: 1.1.

Projection stage: Project records to verdict-consumed fields, scrub configured and discovered credentials, then scrub again at every report and archive write sink.

Sensitive fields and values: access_key, secret_key, x-apikey, authorization, cookie, password, credentials, username

Credential formats: Tenable X-ApiKeys headers, Security Center x-apikey headers, managed credential payloads, URL user information

Reviewed benign exceptions: Stable non-secret resource identifiers and public documentation URLs remain visible unless carried in a credential field.

Integration-specific rules:

- Withhold undocumented error bodies; retain only status, media type, byte length, and allowlisted vendor error codes.
- Remove URL user information, queries, and fragments from evidence and reject off-origin pagination links.
- Unavailable counts, arrays, maps, and negative flags are null rather than fabricated empty values.

|---|---|

### Record schemas

#### finding

- `id`
- `title`
- `severity`
- `status`
- `summary`
- `evidence`
- `framework mappings`

#### collection_marker

- `collected`
- `status`
- `endpoint`
- `error`

#### bundle_result

- `outputDir`
- `zipPath`
- `fileCount`
- `findingCount`
- `errorCount`

#### assessment

- `title or category`
- `summary`
- `findings`
- `errors when collection was partial`

#### pagination_state

- `items or rows seen`
- `reported total when available`
- `pages`
- `truncated`
- `stop reason`

JSON formatting: UTF-8 JSON with two-space indentation and a trailing newline.

Overwrite policy: Allocate <sanitized-platform-host>-audit-bundle and add a numeric suffix when the directory or archive already exists.

Path safety: Resolve beneath the configured output root and reject traversal, unsafe parents, files, and symbolic-link escapes.

Archive pairing: Create <allocated-directory>.zip beside the allocated Tenable audit directory.
